- **__FRONT_END_PLUGIN__-cancel**: Abandon the current branch's work: close its pull request, delete its remote branch, cancel its issue, and run the repo's teardown. Usage: /__FRONT_END_PLUGIN__-cancel [force|help] ([Project-White-Rabbit/agent-flows](https://github.com/Project-White-Rabbit/agent-flows/tree/HEAD/packages/dev/flows/cursor/front-end/cancel))
- **__FRONT_END_PLUGIN__-configure**: Set up or update .agent-flows/config.json, the repo's shared dev configuration: base branch and the integrations it uses for each capability (source control, issue tracker, deployments, error tracking, …). Usage: /__FRONT_END_PLUGIN__-configure [auto | manual] [local | replace] [section | capability ...] ([Project-White-Rabbit/agent-flows](https://github.com/Project-White-Rabbit/agent-flows/tree/HEAD/packages/dev/flows/cursor/front-end/configure))
- **__FRONT_END_PLUGIN__-create-tickets**: File issues from planning notes, a page link, or a sentence, updating issues that already describe the same work. Not for syncing a branch. Usage: /__FRONT_END_PLUGIN__-create-tickets [match|help] [notes URL | pasted notes | sentence] [--yes] [--tracker &lt;entry&gt;] [--tracker-project &lt;name&gt;] ([Project-White-Rabbit/agent-flows](https://github.com/Project-White-Rabbit/agent-flows/tree/HEAD/packages/dev/flows/cursor/front-end/create-tickets))
- **__FRONT_END_PLUGIN__-evaluate**: Assess branch or PR shippability without edits. Use for readiness and risk assessment, not an audit/fix loop. Usage: /__FRONT_END_PLUGIN__-evaluate [full] [record] [PR number or URL | help] ([Project-White-Rabbit/agent-flows](https://github.com/Project-White-Rabbit/agent-flows/tree/HEAD/packages/dev/flows/cursor/front-end/evaluate))
- **__FRONT_END_PLUGIN__-finish**: Land the current branch's pull request (or its stack): wait for CI on the commit that lands, merge it through the repo's source-control or stacking integration, then close out its issue and errors. Only when the user explicitly asks to land or merge the PR, never for a git merge. Usage: /__FRONT_END_PLUGIN__-finish [stack | branch] [squash | merge | rebase] [no-ready] ([Project-White-Rabbit/agent-flows](https://github.com/Project-White-Rabbit/agent-flows/tree/HEAD/packages/dev/flows/cursor/front-end/finish))
- **__FRONT_END_PLUGIN__-preview**: Open the page the current work changed in a browser, on this worktree's dev server (starting it when it isn't running): the workspace app's browser, else this session's browser tools, else the system's. Usage: /__FRONT_END_PLUGIN__-preview [&lt;page path, URL, or description&gt; | help] ([Project-White-Rabbit/agent-flows](https://github.com/Project-White-Rabbit/agent-flows/tree/HEAD/packages/dev/flows/cursor/front-end/preview))
- **__FRONT_END_PLUGIN__-prototype**: Mock up a UI from a description, as a clickable page in the look of the team's design system, and show it to the person wherever this session can (a hosted page, the team's design tool, or a browser), then iterate on it from their feedback. For exploring a UI, not building production components; it writes in the working tree only when the team keeps its mockups there (pages in `prototype.dir`, a record of each in `prototype.index`), and never commits. Usage: /__FRONT_END_PLUGIN__-prototype [&lt;description of the UI&gt; | design [&lt;design link&gt;] | stop | help] ([Project-White-Rabbit/agent-flows](https://github.com/Project-White-Rabbit/agent-flows/tree/HEAD/packages/dev/flows/cursor/front-end/prototype))
- **__FRONT_END_PLUGIN__-ready**: Take finished branch work to a ready, green pull request: scoped local cleanup and checks, commit, publish, mark it ready, and fix CI with high confidence. Not for merging, reviewing, or one-off checks. Usage: /__FRONT_END_PLUGIN__-ready [loop|draft|help] [--review | --no-review] [--skip &lt;steps&gt;] [--ignore-check &lt;pattern&gt;]... - **__FRONT_END_PLUGIN__-server**: Start this worktree's dev server in the background when it isn't running, stop it, list the ones running across the repo's worktrees, or put one started by hand under a watchdog that stops it once its worktree is deleted or nobody uses it. Usage: /__FRONT_END_PLUGIN__-server [up|adopt|restart|stop|down|list|status|help] ([Project-White-Rabbit/agent-flows](https://github.com/Project-White-Rabbit/agent-flows/tree/HEAD/packages/dev/flows/cursor/front-end/server))

([cilly-yllic/.llm](https://github.com/cilly-yllic/.llm/tree/HEAD/skills/en/__pr-review-loop))

Triggered by /add-domain when the registrar is Cloudflare. ([flavien-ia/hypervibe-craft](https://github.com/flavien-ia/hypervibe-craft/tree/HEAD/skills/_dns-cloudflare) · ★55 · 2 copies)
- **_dns-gandi**: Internal - change a Gandi-registered domain's nameservers to point to Cloudflare. Uses Gandi's REST API v5 directly with a Personal Access Token (no CLI install). - **_dns-godaddy-manual**: Internal - guide the user click-by-click to change a GoDaddy-registered domain's nameservers manually. ([flavien-ia/hypervibe-craft](https://github.com/flavien-ia/hypervibe-craft/tree/HEAD/skills/_dns-godaddy-manual) · ★55 · 2 copies)
- **_dns-hostinger**: Internal - change a Hostinger-registered domain's nameservers to point to Cloudflare, via the Hostinger REST API (curl + API token from the Bitwarden vault). No MCP. - **_dns-infomaniak-manual**: Internal - guide the user click-by-click to change an Infomaniak-registered domain's nameservers manually. Used by add-auth for admin credentials. Writes nothing itself; the admin's production password goes, through admin-password.mjs, to a file of the user's downloads folder for them alone, never into the conversation. - **_setup-2fa-admin**: Internal. Sets up 2FA (TOTP) on a hypervibe admin-credentials login - mandatory for the single admin. TOTP code after the password, trusted-device cookie (24h), backup codes, idle auto-logout. The TOTP secret + backup codes are written to the Bitwarden vault (never the chat). Invoked by /add-2fa when the project is in admin mode. Not meant to be invoked directly by users. ([flavien-ia/hypervibe-craft](https://github.com/flavien-ia/hypervibe-craft/tree/HEAD/skills/_setup-2fa-admin) · ★55 · 2 copies)

- **_setup-2fa-users**: Internal. Sets up optional per-user 2FA (TOTP) on a hypervibe user-accounts auth. Login asks for the code after the password only for users who enabled it. Invoked by /add-2fa when the project is in users mode. Claude-piloted (no script) because the account page and signin flow are project-specific. Not meant to be invoked directly by users. - **{{skill:create-ui}}**: 구현 단계의 구현 스킬. breakdown의 UI 작업 하나를 받아 Jetpack Compose 화면, 상태(Contract), 디자인 토큰, 공용 컴포넌트를 작업이 지정한 경로 안에 만들고 화면과 상태마다 프리뷰를 둔다 ([JaesungLeee/sdd-android](https://github.com/JaesungLeee/sdd-android/tree/HEAD/skills/ko/create-ui))
- **{{skill:design-inspector}}**: 구현 단계의 디자인 분석 스킬. spec의 Figma 노드를 열어 구성요소, 구조, 토큰과 치수, 문구, 상태를 뽑아 feature의 분석 파일에 적는다 ([JaesungLeee/sdd-android](https://github.com/JaesungLeee/sdd-android/tree/HEAD/skills/ko/design-inspector))
